also has borrowings and obligation under finance leases and an interest free loan      率計息。本集團亦有借貸及融資租賃責任，及
from a director. Exposure to interest rate risk exists on those balances subject to    來自一名董事之免息貸款。倘有未能預料的不
floating interest rate when there are unexpected adverse interest rate movements.      利利率變動，該等以浮動利率計息之結餘將面
The Group’s policy is to manage its interest rate risk, working within an agreed       對利率風險。本集團的政策為在協定之框架內
framework, to ensure that there are no undue exposures to significant interest         管理其利率風險，以確保不會面對利率大幅變
rate movements and rates are appropriately fixed when necessary.                       動此不合理風險，並於有需要時適當地固定利

obligation under finance leases which are denominated in US$ and EUR. The              貿易應付款項、借貸及融資租賃責任。本集團
Group does not adopt any hedging strategy in the long run but the management           並無採納任何長遠對沖策略，但管理層持續監
continuously monitors the foreign exchange risk exposure and might enter into          察外匯風險並可能按個別情況訂立遠期匯兌合

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                                         企業管治常規

The Company and the Board are devoted to achieving and maintaining high                本公司及董事會致力達致及維持最高水平之企
standard of corporate governance as the Board believes that effective corporate        業管治，董事會相信，行之有效的企業管治常
governance practices are fundamental in enhancing the shareholder value                規對提升股東價值以及保障股東及其他權益相
and safeguarding the interests of the Shareholders and other stakeholders.             關者之利益至關重要。因此，本公司已採納健
Accordingly, the Company has adopted sound corporate governance principles             全之企業管治原則，當中著重優秀之董事會、
that emphasise a quality Board, effective internal control, stringent disclosure       有效之內部監控、嚴謹之披露常規以及對所有
practices and transparency and accountability to all Shareholders.                     股東之透明度及問責性。

The Company has complied with the code provisions set out in the Corporate             本公司已遵守上市規則附錄C1所載之企業管治
Governance Code (the “CG Code”) contained in Appendix C1 to the Listing                守則（
                                                                                         「企業管治守則」   ）之守則條文，惟偏離守則
Rules, except for the deviation from the code provision C.2.1.                         條文第C.2.1條除外。

Under code provision C.2.1, the roles of chairman and chief executive should           根據守則條文第C.2.1條，主席與行政總裁之角
be separate and should not be performed by the same individual in order to             色應有區分，並不應由同一名人士兼任，以確
ensure that there is clear division of responsibilities between the chairman of the    保董事會主席與本公司行政總裁之職責有明確
Board and the chief executive of the Company. The Company has not appointed            區分。本公司並無委任主席，而董事會則領導
a chairman and the Board provides leadership for the Company. Having                   本公司。考慮到本集團於重大時刻之業務營
considered the business operation of the Group at the material time, it is believed    運，本公司認為董事會由經驗豐富之專業人士
that the Board, which consists of experienced professionals, can function              組成，整體可有效運作，而執行董事連同本公
effectively as a whole, while the executive Directors along with other members         司其他高級管理人員則有能力監督本集團在現
of senior management of the Company are capable of overseeing the day-to-day           有穩固企業管治架構下之日常管理工作。
management of the Group under the strong corporate governance structure in
place.

Nevertheless, the Company understands the importance of compliance with the            儘管如此，本公司明白遵守企業管治守則之守
code provision C.2.1 of the CG Code and will continue to review the structure          則條文第C.2.1條十分重要，並將繼續不時檢討
from time to time and consider appointing a chairman/chairlady of the Board as         有關架構，亦考慮在適當時委任董事會主席。
and when appropriate.

The Group commits to continuously improving its corporate governance practices         本集團承諾透過定期檢討持續改善其企業管治
by periodic review to ensure that the Group continues to meet the requirements         常規以確保本集團繼續達到企業管治守則之要
of the CG Code.                                                                        求。

Summary of the Board Diversity Policy                                                 董事會多元化政策摘要

With a view to achieving a sustainable and balanced development, the Company          為達致可持續均衡發展，本公司視董事會層面
sees increasing diversity at the Board level as an essential element in supporting    日益多元化為支持其達到策略目標及可持續發

business

take legal actions as appropriate. All exposures are subject to regular review and       品之權利或採取法律行動（如適用）。所有風險
assessment for possible impairment.                                                      敞口均須定期檢討及評估減值之可能性。

Share Award Scheme                                                                    股份獎勵計劃

The Company has adopted the Share Award Scheme on 24 April 2020. Unless               本公司於二零二零年四月二十四日採納股份獎
early terminated by the Board, the Share Award Scheme shall be valid and              勵計劃。除非董事會提前終止，否則股份獎勵
effective for a term of 10 years commencing on the adoption date (i.e. 24 April       計劃之有效期自採納日期（即二零二零年四月
2020) and will expire on 23 April 2030. The remaining life of the share award         二十四日）起為期10年及將於二零三零年四月
scheme is 6 years.                                                                    二十三日到期。股份獎勵計劃的剩餘期限為6
                                                                                      年。

The Share Award Scheme serves as an incentive measure to recognise the                股份獎勵計劃作為獎勵措施旨在確認選定參與
contributions by selected participants and to provide them with incentives in         者作出之貢獻，並為彼等提供獎勵以留住彼
order to retain them; and to attract the right talents for promoting the future       等；以及吸引合適人才以促進本集團未來發展
development and expansion of the Group, with a view to achieving the objective        與擴充業務，目的為透過鼓勵參與者為本集團
of increasing the value of the Group by motivating the participants to strive for     持續經營以及未來發展與擴充業務而努力，並
the continual operation and future development and expansion of the Group and         使參與者與本公司股東利益相符，從而實現提
aligning the interests of the participants and the shareholders of the Company.       升本集團價值此目標。

director (including executive director, non-executive director and independent        酌情認為已經或將會對本集團作出貢獻之本公
non-executive director), employee (whether full time or part time), officer, agent    司或其任何附屬公司或其任何聯屬公司任何董
or consultant of the Company or any of its subsidiaries or any of its affiliates or   事（包括執行董事、非執行董事及獨立非執行董
any other person as determined by the Board, whom the Board considers, in its         事）、僱員（不論全職或兼職）、高級職員、代理
absolute discretion, has contributed or will contribute to the Group.                 人或顧問或董事會決定之其他人士。

Scheme.                                                                               並沒有配額上限。

not a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will always         義務。合理保證是高水準的保證，但不能保證

or error and are considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they      誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐

       Practice Statement 2 Disclosure of Accounting Policies                              財務報告準則實務報告第2號（修訂本）
                                                                                          「會計政策之披露」之影響

       The Group has applied the amendments for the first time in the current             本集團於本年度首次應用該修訂本。香港
       year. HKAS 1 Presentation of Financial Statements is amended to replace            會計準則第1號「財務報表呈列」，以「重大
       all instances of the term “significant accounting policies” with “material         會計政策資料」取代所有「主要會計政策」一
       accounting policy information”. Accounting policy information is material          詞。倘會計政策資料與一個實體之財務報
       if, when considered together with other information included in an entity’s        表所載的其他資料一併考慮時，可合理預
       financial statements, it can reasonably be expected to influence decisions         期會影響一般用途財務報表主要使用者根
       that the primary users of general purpose financial statements make on the         據該等財務報表作出的決定，則會計政策
       basis of those financial statements.                                               資料為重大。

       The amendments also clarify that accounting policy information may be              該等修訂本亦澄清，會計政策資料可因相
       material because of the nature of the related transactions, other events           關交易的性質、其他事件或條件而屬重
       or conditions, even if the amounts are immaterial. However, not all                大，即使金額並不重大。然而，並非所有
       accounting policy information relating to material transactions, other             與重大交易、其他事件或條件有關的會計
       events or conditions is itself material. If an entity chooses to disclose          政策資料本身均屬重大。倘實體選擇披露
       immaterial accounting policy information, such information must not                非重大會計政策資料，則該等資料不得掩
       obscure material accounting policy information.                                    蓋重大會計政策資料。

       3.1 Basis of preparation of consolidated financial                                     3.1 編製綜合財務報表之基準
           statements

              The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in                            綜合財務報表已按香港會計師公會
              accordance with HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA. For the purpose                           頒佈之香港財務報告準則編製。就
              of preparation of the consolidated financial statements, information                   編製綜合財務報表而言，如果合理
              is considered material if such information is reasonably expected                      預期此類資料會影響主要使用者所
              to influence decisions made by primary users. In addition, the                         作的決策，則該資料被視為重大。
              consolidated financial statements include applicable disclosures                       此外，綜合財務報表包括上市規則
              required by the Listing Rules and by the Hong Kong Companies                           及香港公司條例之適用披露規定。
              Ordinance.

              historical cost basis, except for investment properties and certain                    所闡釋，除投資物業及若干金融工
              financial instruments that are measured at fair values at the end                      具於各呈報期末按公平值計量外，
              of each reporting period, as explained in the material accounting                      綜合財務報表乃按歷史成本基準編
              policy information set out below.                                                      製。

              Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the                            歷史成本一般以交換貨品及服務時
              consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                                所付代價之公平值為基準。

              Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or                     公平值為於計量日期市場參與者在
              paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market                  有序交易中出售資產所收取或轉讓
              participants at the measurement date, regardless of whether that                       負債所支付之價格，而不論該價格
              price is directly observable or estimated using another valuation                      為直接可觀察或使用其他估值技術
              technique.                                                                             估計得出。

              the customer.                                                                            益。

              A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a bundle                       履約責任指一項明確貨品或服務（或
              of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct goods or                  一批貨品或服務）或一系列大致相同
              services that are substantially the same.                                                的明確貨品或服務。

              time by reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction of                       權為隨時間轉移，而收益則參考完
              the relevant performance obligation if one of the following criteria is                  全履行相關履約責任的進展情況隨
              met:                                                                                     時間確認：

              •      the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the                             •   客戶於本集團履約時同時收取
                     benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the Group                             及消耗本集團履約所提供的利
                     performs;                                                                             益；

              •      the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset that                         •   本集團的履約創造或提升客戶
                     the customer controls as the Group performs; or                                       於本集團履約時控制的資產；
                                                                                                           或

              •      the Group’s performance does not create an asset with                             •   本集團的履約並無創造對本集
                     an alternative use to the Group and the Group has an                                  團具有替代用途的資產，而本
                     enforceable right to payment for performance completed to                             集團擁有強制執行權收取至今
                     date.                                                                                 已履約部分的款項。

              Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the                             否則，收益於客戶獲得明確商品或
              customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.                                服務控制權時確認。

              income tax expense.                                                               支的總和。

              not been adjusted.                                                              及該資產（或現金產生單位）特有的風

                                                                                                      該債務工具的信貸風險偏低。

                     Group uses a practical expedient in estimating ECL on                          而釐定。本集團經考慮過往信

       which the estimate is revised if the revision affects only that period, or in        之修訂會於估計被修訂之期間確認，或倘

       including past due dates and probability of default, of each borrower                     之前瞻性資料（例如宏觀經濟數據因素，
       and reasonable and supportable forward-looking information (such as                       包括國內生產總值（
                                                                                                         「國內生產總值」）增長
       macroeconomic factors including Gross Domestic Product (“GDP”)                            及失業率，並根據經濟環境前景的不同情
       growth and unemployment rate with adjustment on different scenarios of                    況作出調整），獨立評估應收借款人之貸
       economic environment prospect) that is available without undue cost or                    款及利息以及應收企業票據。
       effort.

       Each borrower is assigned a risk grading under internal credit ratings ,                  各借款人根據內部信貸評級獲分配風險等
       with reference to its individual external credit rating where available, to               級，參考其個人外部信用評級（如有），以
       calculate the ECL, taking into consideration of the estimates of expected                 計算預期信貸虧損，同時經考慮估計預期
       cash shortfalls which are driven by the estimates of probability of                       現金短缺，乃根據估計違約可能性以及預
       default and the amount and timing of cash flows that are expected from                    期抵押品止贖（如有）之現金流的金額及時
       foreclosure on the collateral (if any) less the costs of selling the collateral.          間減去出售抵押品之成本計算。於各報告
       At every reporting date, the financial background, financial condition and                日期，各借款人的財務背景、財務狀況及
       historical settlement records of each borrower are reassessed and changes                 過往還款記錄會重新評估，並考慮前瞻性
       in the forward–looking information are considered. The management                         資料的變動。管理層進一步評估違約風險
       further assesses the amount of exposure of default by assessing the                       金額，以評估本集團面臨之信貸減值應收
       potential loss in view of the credit risk on credit-impaired loan and interest            貸款及利息之信貸風險導致之潛在損失，
       receivables to which the Group is exposed and enables the Group to                        並促使本集團立即採取合適糾正行動。於
       take appropriate corrective actions promptly. In assessing the amount of                  評估違約風險之金額時，本集團考慮抵押
       exposure of default, the Group takes into account the timing of cash flows                品止贖之預期現金流之時間減出售抵押品
       that are expected from foreclosure on the collateral less the costs of selling            之成本。預期信貸虧損之撥備容易受到估
       the collateral. The provision of ECL is sensitive to changes in estimates.                計變動之影響。

       Impairment assessment of properties for development                                發展中物業減值評估

       Properties for development are stated at costs less impairment, if any. In         發展中物業按成本減去減值（如有）列賬。
       determining whether it is impaired, the Group has to exercise judgment             於釐定其是否減值時，本集團須進行判斷
       and make estimation, particularly in assessing: (1) whether an event has           及作出估計，尤其在評估：(1)是否有事件
       occurred or any indicators that may affect the asset value; (2) whether the        已發生或有任何跡象可能影響資產價值；
       carrying value of an asset can be supported by the recoverable amount;             (2)資產賬面值是否能夠以可收回金額支
       and (3) the appropriate key assumptions to be applied in estimating the            持；及(3)於估計可收回金額時適用的適當
       recoverable amounts including cash flow projections and an appropriate             關鍵假設，包括現金流預測及適當的貼現
       discount rate. Change in the assumptions and estimates, including                  率。更改假設及估計（包括貼現率、未來
       discount rates, future selling prices and future construction costs in the         銷售價格及未來建築成本）可能會顯著影
       cash flow projections, could materiality affect the recoverable amounts.           響可收回金額。

       As at 31 March 2024, the carrying amounts of properties for development             於二零二四年三月三十一日，須進行減值
       subject to impairment assessment was HK$326 million (2023: HK$352                   評估的發展中物業賬面金額為326百萬港元
       million). No impairment losses have been recognized (2023: nil).                   （二零二三年：352百萬港元）。並無確認減
                                                                                           值損失
                                                                                             （二零二三年：無） 。

       may be affected by the global economic challenges, the Group performed             地位於發展中國家，可能受到全球經濟挑
       impairment assessment on its interests in this associate. Determining              戰的影響，本集團對於該聯營公司之權益
       whether impairment loss should be recognised requires an estimation of             進行了減值評估。釐定是否應確認減值虧
       the recoverable amount of the relevant associate which is the higher of            損要求估計相關聯營公司的可收回金額，

       Credit risk and impairment assessment                                                    信貸風險及減值評估

       Credit risk refers to the risk that the Group’s counterparties default on                信貸風險指本集團對手方就其合約責任違
       their contractual obligations resulting in financial losses to the Group.                約導致本集團遭受財務損失的風險。本集
       The Group’s credit risk exposures are primarily attributable to trade                    團的信貸風險主要歸因於貿易應收款項、
       receivables, other receivables and deposits, loan receivables, finance                   其他應收款項及訂金、應收貸款、融資租

       balances and debt instruments at FVTPL. The Group does not hold any                      存款、銀行結餘及以公平值計量且其變化
       collateral or other credit enhancements to cover its credit risks associated             計入損益之債務工具。本集團並無持有任
       with its financial assets except that the credit risks associated with certain           何抵押品或其他信貸增強工具以覆蓋與其

       In assessing whether the credit risk has increased significantly since                    於評估自初始確認後信貸風險是否顯著增
       initial recognition, the Group compares the risk of default occurring                     加時，本集團將於報告日期就金融工具發
       on the financial instrument at the reporting date with the risk perceived                 生的違約風險與初始確認日期感知到的風
       at the date of initial recognition. In making this assessment, the loan                   險進行比較。進行此評估時，本集團管理
       receivables from borrowers are assessed individually by the management                    層乃基於各借款人的財務背景、財務狀況
       of the Group, based on the financial background, financial condition and                  及過往結付記錄（包括逾期日期及違約概
       historical settlement records, including past due dates and probability of                率），以及在不付出過多成本或努力的情
       default, of each borrower and reasonable and supportable forward-looking                  況下可獲得的合理且可提供支持依據的前
       information that is available without undue cost or effort. Each borrower                 瞻性資料，對應收借款人的貸款分別進行
       is assigned a risk grading under internal credit ratings to calculate the                 評估。根據內部信用評級對各借款人進行
       ECL, taking into consideration the estimates of expected cash shortfalls                  風險分級，以計算預期信貸虧損，當中計
       which are driven by the estimates of probability of default and expected                  及對違約概率及預期違約損失所帶來的預
       loss given default, including taking into account the amount and timing                   期現金短缺之估計，包括計及抵押品取消
       of cash flows that are expected from foreclosure on the collateral (if any)               贖回權（如有）預期產生的現金流之金額及
       less the costs of selling the collateral. At every reporting date, the financial          時間減去出售抵押品的成本。於每個報告
       background, financial condition and historical settlement records of each                 日期，會對各借款人的財務背景、財務狀
       borrower are reassessed and changes in the forward-looking information                    況及過往結付記錄進行重新評估，並考慮
       are considered.                                                                           前瞻性資料的變化。

       financial reporting purposes.                                                          公平值計量。

       In estimating the fair values, the Group uses market-observable data to the            於估計公平值時，本集團會使用可取得的
       extent it is available. For instruments with significant unobservable inputs           市場可觀察數據。針對含有第3級重大不
       under Level 3, the Group engages third party qualified valuers to perform              可觀察輸入數據之工具，本集團委聘第三
       the valuation.                                                                         方合資格估值師進行估值。

       Some of the Group’s financial assets and financial liabilities are measured            本集團部分金融資產及金融負債於各報告
       at fair values at the end of each reporting period.                                    期末按公平值計量。

       The directors consider that the carrying amounts of financial assets and               董事認為，於綜合財務報表按攤銷成本確
       financial liabilities at amortised cost recognised in the consolidated                 認之金融資產及金融負債之賬面值與彼等
       financial statements approximate to their fair values.                                 之公平值相若。
